## This Week in Security: Your News Briefing

Welcome to your weekly security roundup. We've tracked down the **10 most important stories** this
week—the ones everyone's talking about, from critical threats to emerging trends that could shape
your security posture. Leading the news this week is **CVE-2024-6100**, which has sparked
conversation across 4 sources. Meanwhile, the industry is closely tracking **Threat Modeling and
Social Issues** with 4 mentions, along with emerging details on **CVE-2025-8045**, **CVE-2026-21060
| Samsung Devices up to R(11.0) input validation**, and **CVE-2026-18468 | Login & Register Forms
Plugin up to 4.0.1 on...**. Here's the full breakdown of what you need to know.

### 🚨 Critical Threats This Week

First, the stories that demand your immediate attention:

**1. CVE-2024-6100**
   Mentioned across 4 industry sources this week. Currently trending CVE - Hype Score: 15 - Type Confusion in V8 in Google Chrome prior to 126.
   [Get the details →](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2024-6100)

**2. Threat Modeling and Social Issues**
   Mentioned across 4 industry sources this week. For Data Breach Today, I spoke with Anna Delaney about threat modeling for issues that are in the news right now.
   [Get the details →](https://shostack.org/blog/threat-modeling-and-social-issues/)

**3. CVE-2025-8045**
   Mentioned across 2 industry sources this week. Currently trending CVE - Hype Score: 8 - Use After Free vulnerability in Arm Ltd Valhall GPU Kernel Driver, Arm Ltd Arm .
   [Get the details →](https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/CVE-2025-8045)

### 🛠️ Tools, Updates & Releases

New capabilities and releases worth knowing about:

**1. Promoting AI Security — Acting Assistant Privacy Commissioner Speaks at the GenA.I. Symposium**
   . Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](http://www.pcpd.org.hk/english/whatsnew/20251031.html)

**2. Ransom Cartel creator sentenced to 16 years in prison**
   Maksim Silnikau participated in cybercrime since at least 2005. Referenced in 3 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://cyberscoop.com/ransom-cartel-creator-sentenced-to-16-years-in-prison/)

**3. OpenAI is finally killing ChatGPT’s text chat limits for free users**
   OpenAI has just announced that it’s now removing text chat usage limits for ChatGPT Free and Go acco. Referenced in 2 stories this week.
   [Explore →](https://www.pcworld.com/article/3208588/openai-is-finally-killing-chatgpt-text-chat-limits-for-free-users.html)

### What You Should Do Next

**Monitor these** in your environment next week:
- Any new CVE announcements related to systems you operate
- Emerging attack techniques being discussed in the community
- Updates and patches for tools your team uses

**Have a look at** the full deep-dives in the trending stories below. Each one provides context that could inform your security decisions this week.
